<!-- components/admin/UsuarioFormModal.vue -->
<!-- Formulario de alta/edición de usuarios del panel. Es "tonto": no llama a la
     API, sólo valida y emite el payload para que lo guarde la página. -->
<template>
  <Modal
    :model-value="modelValue"
    :title="esEdicion ? 'Editar usuario' : 'Nuevo usuario'"
    size="xl"
    @update:model-value="cerrar"
  >
    <form @submit.prevent="enviar" class="space-y-5">
      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <!-- Email -->
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Correo electrónico *</label>
          <input
            v-model.trim="form.email"
            type="email"
            :disabled="esEdicion"
            class="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-md text-sm disabled:bg-gray-100 disabled:text-gray-500"
            :class="errores.email ? 'border-red-400' : 'border-gray-300'"
            placeholder="usuario@senado.bo"
          />
          <p v-if="errores.email" class="mt-1 text-xs text-red-600">{{ errores.email }}</p>
          <p v-else-if="esEdicion" class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">
            El email no se puede modificar.
          </p>
        </div>

        <!-- Contraseña (sólo al crear) -->
        <div v-if="!esEdicion">
          <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Contraseña *</label>
          <input
            v-model="form.password"
            type="text"
            autocomplete="new-password"
            class="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border rounded-md text-sm"
            :class="errores.password ? 'border-red-400' : 'border-gray-300'"
            placeholder="Mínimo 8 caracteres"
          />
          <p v-if="errores.password" class="mt-1 text-xs text-red-600">{{ errores.password }}</p>
          <p v-else class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">
            Al menos 8 caracteres, una mayúscula, una minúscula y un número.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <!-- Rol -->
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Rol *</label>
          <select
            v-model="form.role"
            :disabled="bloqueaRol"
            class="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md text-sm disabled:bg-gray-100 disabled:text-gray-500"
          >
            <option v-for="r in opcionesRol" :key="r.id" :value="r.id">
              {{ r.label }} ({{ r.id }})
            </option>
          </select>
          <p v-if="bloqueaRol" class="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-600">{{ motivoBloqueoRol }}</p>
          <p v-else class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">
            {{ descripcionRolSeleccionado }}
          </p>
        </div>

        <!-- Estado -->
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Estado *</label>
          <select
            v-model="form.status"
            class="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md text-sm"
          >
            <option v-for="s in USER_STATUSES" :key="s.id" :value="s.id">
              {{ s.label }} ({{ s.id }})
            </option>
          </select>
          <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-400">
            Sólo los usuarios <strong>Activo</strong> pueden iniciar sesión.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="border-t border-gray-100 pt-4">
        <p class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-3">Datos del perfil (opcional)</p>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm text-gray-600">Nombres</label>
            <input
              v-model.trim="form.profile.firstName"
              type="text"
              class="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md text-sm"
            />
            <p v-if="errores.firstName" class="mt-1 text-xs text-red-600">{{ errores.firstName }}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm text-gray-600">Apellidos</label>
            <input
              v-model.trim="form.profile.lastName"
              type="text"
              class="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md text-sm"
            />
            <p v-if="errores.lastName" class="mt-1 text-xs text-red-600">{{ errores.lastName }}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm text-gray-600">Cédula de identidad</label>
            <input
              v-model.trim="form.profile.ci"
              type="text"
              class="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md text-sm"
            />
            <p v-if="errores.ci" class="mt-1 text-xs text-red-600">{{ errores.ci }}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm text-gray-600">Cargo</label>
            <input
              v-model.trim="form.profile.position"
              type="text"
              class="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md text-sm"
              placeholder="Ej: Responsable de Prensa"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm text-gray-600">Unidad / Departamento</label>
            <input
              v-model.trim="form.profile.department"
              type="text"
              class="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-md text-sm"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div v-if="serverError" class="rounded-md bg-red-50 border border-red-200 p-3">
        <p class="text-sm text-red-800">{{ serverError }}</p>
      </div>

      <div v-if="errorGeneral" class="rounded-md bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 p-3">
        <p class="text-sm text-amber-800">{{ errorGeneral }}</p>
      </div>

      <div class="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          @click="cerrar(false)"
          class="px-4 py-2 border rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 text-sm"
        >
          Cancelar
        </button>
        <button
          type="submit"
          :disabled="saving"
          class="px-4 py-2 bg-primary-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-primary-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-sm"
        >
          {{ saving ? 'Guardando...' : (esEdicion ? 'Guardar cambios' : 'Crear usuario') }}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  </Modal>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, reactive, ref, watch } from 'vue'
import Modal from '~/components/ui/Modal.vue'
import {
  USER_STATUSES,
  assignableRoles as assignableRolesFor,
  roleInfo,
} from '~/utils/roles'
import type { CreateUserPayload, UpdateUserPayload } from '~/composables/useUsers'
import type { Role, User, UserStatus } from '~/types/auth'

const props = defineProps<{
  modelValue: boolean
  /** null = crear */
  user: User | null
  actorRole: Role | null
  /** id del usuario logueado (para no cambiarse el rol a sí mismo) */
  currentUserId?: string
  saving?: boolean
  serverError?: string
}>()

const emit = defineEmits<{
  (e: 'update:modelValue', value: boolean): void
  (e: 'submit', payload: CreateUserPayload | UpdateUserPayload): void
}>()

const esEdicion = computed(() => !!props.user)

const form = reactive({
  email: '',
  password: '',
  role: 'EDITOR' as Role,
  status: 'ACTIVE' as UserStatus,
  profile: {
    firstName: '',
    lastName: '',
    ci: '',
    position: '',
    department: '',
  },
})

/** Rol sugerido al crear: EDITOR si se puede, si no el menos privilegiado disponible. */
function rolPorDefecto(): Role {
  const permitidos = assignableRolesFor(props.actorRole)
  if (permitidos.includes('EDITOR')) return 'EDITOR'
  return permitidos[permitidos.length - 1] || 'VIEWER'
}

const errores = reactive<Record<string, string>>({})
const errorGeneral = ref('')

const opcionesRol = computed(() => {
  const permitidos = assignableRolesFor(props.actorRole)
  // Si el usuario editado tiene un rol que el actor no puede asignar, igual
  // mostramos la opción para no mentir con el valor actual.
  const actual = props.user?.role
  if (actual && !permitidos.includes(actual)) {
    return [actual, ...permitidos].map(id => roleInfo(id) || { id, label: id })
  }
  return permitidos.map(id => roleInfo(id) || { id, label: id })
})

const descripcionRolSeleccionado = computed(
  () => roleInfo(form.role)?.description || ''
)

const esUsuarioActual = computed(
  () => !!props.currentUserId && String(props.user?._id) === String(props.currentUserId)
)

const bloqueaRol = computed(() => {
  if (esUsuarioActual.value) return true
  const actual = props.user?.role
  if (actual && !assignableRolesFor(props.actorRole).includes(actual)) return true
  return false
})

const motivoBloqueoRol = computed(() => {
  if (esUsuarioActual.value) {
    return 'No podés cambiarte el rol a vos mismo: pedile a otro administrador.'
  }
  return 'Tu rol no puede modificar a un usuario con ese rol.'
})

// Sincronizar el formulario cada vez que se abre
watch(
  () => props.modelValue,
  (abierto) => {
    if (!abierto) return
    limpiarErrores()

    const u = props.user
    form.email = u?.email || ''
    form.password = ''
    form.role = (u?.role || rolPorDefecto()) as Role
    form.status = (u?.status || 'ACTIVE') as UserStatus
    form.profile.firstName = u?.profile?.firstName || ''
    form.profile.lastName = u?.profile?.lastName || ''
    form.profile.ci = u?.profile?.ci || ''
    form.profile.position = u?.profile?.position || ''
    form.profile.department = u?.profile?.department || ''
  },
  { immediate: true }
)

function limpiarErrores() {
  Object.keys(errores).forEach(k => delete errores[k])
  errorGeneral.value = ''
}

function validar(): boolean {
  limpiarErrores()

  if (!form.email) {
    errores.email = 'El email es obligatorio'
  } else if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(form.email)) {
    errores.email = 'El email no es válido'
  }

  if (!esEdicion.value) {
    if (!form.password) {
      errores.password = 'La contraseña es obligatoria'
    } else if (form.password.length < 8) {
      errores.password = 'Debe tener al menos 8 caracteres'
    } else if (!/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\d)/.test(form.password)) {
      errores.password = 'Debe tener una mayúscula, una minúscula y un número'
    }
  }

  if (form.profile.firstName && form.profile.firstName.length < 2) {
    errores.firstName = 'Mínimo 2 caracteres'
  }
  if (form.profile.lastName && form.profile.lastName.length < 2) {
    errores.lastName = 'Mínimo 2 caracteres'
  }
  if (form.profile.ci && form.profile.ci.length < 5) {
    errores.ci = 'La cédula debe tener al menos 5 caracteres'
  }

  if (!assignableRolesFor(props.actorRole).includes(form.role) && !esEdicion.value) {
    errorGeneral.value = 'No tenés permiso para crear usuarios con ese rol.'
  }

  return Object.keys(errores).length === 0 && !errorGeneral.value
}

function cerrar(value = false) {
  emit('update:modelValue', value)
}

function enviar() {
  if (!validar()) return

  const profile = {
    firstName: form.profile.firstName || undefined,
    lastName: form.profile.lastName || undefined,
    ci: form.profile.ci || undefined,
    position: form.profile.position || undefined,
    department: form.profile.department || undefined,
  }

  if (esEdicion.value) {
    const payload: UpdateUserPayload = {
      status: form.status,
      profile,
    }
    if (!bloqueaRol.value) payload.role = form.role
    emit('submit', payload)
  } else {
    emit('submit', {
      email: form.email,
      password: form.password,
      role: form.role,
      status: form.status,
      profile,
    } as CreateUserPayload)
  }
}
</script>

<!-- components/ui/Modal.vue -->
<template>
  <Teleport to="body">
    <Transition
      enter-active-class="transition duration-200 ease-out"
      enter-from-class="opacity-0"
      enter-to-class="opacity-100"
      leave-active-class="transition duration-150 ease-in"
      leave-from-class="opacity-100"
      leave-to-class="opacity-0"
    >
      <!-- 🔥 z-[99999] para que nada lo tape -->
      <div v-if="modelValue" class="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] flex items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-0">
        
        <!-- Fondo oscuro clickeable -->
        <div 
          class="absolute inset-0 bg-gray-500/75 transition-opacity" 
          aria-hidden="true" 
          @click="$emit('update:modelValue', false)"
        ></div>

        <!-- Contenedor del Modal -->
        <div 
          class="relative bg-white rounded-lg text-left overflow-hidden shadow-xl transform transition-all w-full flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]"
          :class="{
            'sm:max-w-lg': size === 'lg',
            'sm:max-w-xl': size === 'xl',
            'sm:max-w-2xl': size === '2xl',
            'sm:max-w-4xl': size === '4xl'
          }"
          @click.stop
        >
          <!-- Header -->
          <div class="bg-gray-50 px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-200 flex-shrink-0">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">
                {{ title }}
              </h3>
              <button
                @click="$emit('update:modelValue', false)"
                class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 transition-colors"
              >
                <span class="text-2xl leading-none">&times;</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Contenido con scroll -->
          <div class="px-6 py-4 overflow-y-auto">
            <slot />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Transition>
  </Teleport>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
defineProps<{
  modelValue: boolean
  title?: string
  size?: 'lg' | 'xl' | '2xl' | '4xl'
}>()

defineEmits<{
  (e: 'update:modelValue', value: boolean): void
}>()
</script>

composables\useApi.ts 
================================= 
 
// composables/useApi.ts
import { useAuthStore } from '~/stores/auth'

export const useApi = () => {
  const authStore = useAuthStore()

  const getHeaders = () => {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    }
    
    const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
    console.log('🔑 [useApi.getHeaders] Token desde localStorage:', token ? `${token.substring(0, 30)}...` : 'NO HAY TOKEN')
    
    if (token) {
      headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`
      console.log('🔑 [useApi.getHeaders] Header Authorization agregado')
    } else {
      console.log('🔴 [useApi.getHeaders] NO hay token en localStorage')
    }
    
    return headers
  }

  const handleResponse = async <T>(response: Response, url: string): Promise<T> => {
    console.log(`🔵 [useApi] Respuesta de ${url}: status ${response.status}`)
    
    if (response.status === 401) {
      console.log('🔴 [useApi] Token expirado, redirigiendo a login')
      localStorage.removeItem('auth_token')
      localStorage.removeItem('user')
      authStore.clearAuth()
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
        window.location.href = '/auth/login?session=expired'
      }
      throw new Error('Sesión expirada')
    }
    
    if (!response.ok) {
      const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}))
      console.log('🔴 [useApi] Error:', data)
      throw new Error(data.message || `Error ${response.status}`)
    }
    
    const data = await response.json()
    console.log('🔵 [useApi] Respuesta exitosa')
    return data.data || data
  }

  return {
    get: async <T>(url: string): Promise<T> => {
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi] GET ${url}`)
      
      const cacheBuster = `_=${Date.now()}`
      const separator = url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?'
      const finalUrl = `${url}${separator}${cacheBuster}`
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi] GET con anti-caché: ${finalUrl}`)
      
      const headers = getHeaders()
      const response = await fetch(finalUrl, {
        method: 'GET',
        headers,
        cache: 'no-store',
      })
      return handleResponse<T>(response, url)
    },

    post: async <T>(url: string, body: any): Promise<T> => {
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi] POST ${url}`)
      console.log('🔵 [useApi] Body:', body)
      const headers = getHeaders()
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      })
      return handleResponse<T>(response, url)
    },

    put: async <T>(url: string, body: any): Promise<T> => {
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi.put] ========== PUT ==========`)
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi.put] URL: ${url}`)
      console.log('🔵 [useApi.put] Body recibido:', body)
      
      const headers = getHeaders()
      
      // Asegurar que las fechas están en formato ISO
      if (body.publishedAt) {
        const dateObj = new Date(body.publishedAt)
        if (!isNaN(dateObj.getTime())) {
          body.publishedAt = dateObj.toISOString()
        }
      }
      
      if (body.scheduledFor) {
        const dateObj = new Date(body.scheduledFor)
        if (!isNaN(dateObj.getTime())) {
          body.scheduledFor = dateObj.toISOString()
        }
      }
      
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify(body)
      })
      
      return handleResponse<T>(response, url)
    },

    // 🔥 MÉTODO PATCH - AGREGADO
    patch: async <T>(url: string, body: any): Promise<T> => {
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi.patch] ========== PATCH ==========`)
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi.patch] URL: ${url}`)
      console.log('🔵 [useApi.patch] Body:', body)
      
      const headers = getHeaders()
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      })
      
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi.patch] Status: ${response.status}`)
      
      const data = await response.json()
      console.log('🔵 [useApi.patch] Respuesta:', data)
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi.patch] ========== FIN PATCH ==========`)
      
      return data.data || data
    },

    del: async <T>(url: string): Promise<T> => {
      console.log(`🔵 [useApi] DELETE ${url}`)
      const headers = getHeaders()
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers,
      })
      return handleResponse<T>(response, url)
    },
  }
}

composables\useAuditoria.ts 
================================= 
 
// composables/useAuditoria.ts
import { useApi } from '~/composables/useApi'

// ============================================
// TIPOS
// ============================================

export type ModuloAuditoria =
  | 'poa-uai'
  | 'auditorias-ejecutadas'
  | 'informes-actividades'
  | 'otras-actividades'

export type EstadoAuditoria = 'Publicado' | 'En Revisión' | 'Borrador'

export interface AuditoriaCategoria {
  _id?: string
  modulo: ModuloAuditoria
  key: string
  nombre: string
  icono: string
  orden: number
  activo: boolean
  documentosCount?: number
}

export interface AuditoriaDocumento {
  _id: string
  modulo: ModuloAuditoria
  categoria: string
  titulo: string
  descripcion: string
  gestion: number
  url: string | null
  estado: EstadoAuditoria
  activo: boolean
  orden: number
  migrado?: boolean
  urlOriginal?: string
  createdAt?: string
  updatedAt?: string
}

export interface AuditoriaPOA {
  _id: string
  anio: number
  titulo: string
  fecha: string
  estado: EstadoAuditoria
  activo: boolean
  pdfUrl: string | null
  createdAt?: string
  updatedAt?: string
}

// ============================================
// COMPOSABLE
// ============================================

export const useAuditoria = () => {
  const { get, post, put, del, patch } = useApi()
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const baseUrl = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

  // ============================================
  // 📡 PÚBLICO
  // ============================================

  const getDocumentosPublicos = async (modulo: ModuloAuditoria) => {
    const url = `${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}`
    const response = await fetch(url)
    const result = await response.json()
    if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.message)
    return result.data
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🔐 ADMIN - DOCUMENTOS
  // ============================================

  const getDocumentosAdmin = async (
    modulo: ModuloAuditoria,
    params?: {
      categoria?: string
      gestion?: number
      search?: string
      incluirInactivos?: boolean
    }
  ) => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams()
    if (params?.categoria) query.append('categoria', params.categoria)
    if (params?.gestion) query.append('gestion', params.gestion.toString())
    if (params?.search) query.append('search', params.search)
    if (params?.incluirInactivos) query.append('incluirInactivos', 'true')

    const url = `${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/admin?${query.toString()}`
    return await get<{ documentos: AuditoriaDocumento[]; total: number }>(url)
  }

  const getDocumentoById = async (
    modulo: ModuloAuditoria,
    id: string
  ): Promise<AuditoriaDocumento> => {
    return await get<AuditoriaDocumento>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/${id}`)
  }

  const createDocumento = async (
    modulo: ModuloAuditoria,
    data: Partial<AuditoriaDocumento>
  ) => {
    return await post<AuditoriaDocumento>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}`, data)
  }

  const updateDocumento = async (
    modulo: ModuloAuditoria,
    id: string,
    data: Partial<AuditoriaDocumento>
  ) => {
    return await put<AuditoriaDocumento>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/${id}`, data)
  }

  const deleteDocumento = async (modulo: ModuloAuditoria, id: string) => {
    return await del(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/${id}`)
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🔐 ADMIN - CATEGORÍAS
  // ============================================

  const getCategorias = async (
    modulo: ModuloAuditoria,
    incluirInactivos = false
  ): Promise<AuditoriaCategoria[]> => {
    const url = `${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/categorias?incluirInactivos=${incluirInactivos}`
    const result = await get<AuditoriaCategoria[]>(url)
    return result
  }

  const createCategoria = async (
    modulo: ModuloAuditoria,
    data: Partial<AuditoriaCategoria>
  ) => {
    return await post<AuditoriaCategoria>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/categorias`, data)
  }

  const updateCategoria = async (
    modulo: ModuloAuditoria,
    key: string,
    data: Partial<AuditoriaCategoria>
  ) => {
    return await put<AuditoriaCategoria>(
      `${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/categorias/${key}`,
      data
    )
  }

  const deleteCategoria = async (modulo: ModuloAuditoria, key: string) => {
    return await del(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/categorias/${key}`)
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🔐 ADMIN - POA-UAI
  // ============================================

  const getPOAs = async (): Promise<AuditoriaPOA[]> => {
    const result = await get<{ documentos: AuditoriaPOA[] }>(
      `${baseUrl}/auditoria/poa-uai/admin`
    )
    return result.documentos || []
  }

  const getPOAById = async (id: string): Promise<AuditoriaPOA> => {
    return await get<AuditoriaPOA>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/poa-uai/${id}`)
  }

  const createPOA = async (data: Partial<AuditoriaPOA>) => {
    return await post<AuditoriaPOA>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/poa-uai`, data)
  }

  const updatePOA = async (id: string, data: Partial<AuditoriaPOA>) => {
    return await put<AuditoriaPOA>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/poa-uai/${id}`, data)
  }

  const deletePOA = async (id: string) => {
    return await del(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/poa-uai/${id}`)
  }

  const togglePOAActivo = async (id: string, activo: boolean) => {
    return await patch<AuditoriaPOA>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/poa-uai/${id}/toggle`, { activo })
  }

  // ============================================
  // 📤 UPLOADS
  // ============================================

  const uploadPDF = async (
    file: File,
    modulo: ModuloAuditoria
  ): Promise<{
    url: string
    filename: string
    originalName: string
    size: number
  }> => {
    const formData = new FormData()
    formData.append('pdf', file)

    const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/upload-pdf`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      body: formData,
    })
    const result = await response.json()
    if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.message)
    return result.data
  }

  // ============================================
  // 📊 ESTADÍSTICAS
  // ============================================

  const getStats = async (modulo: ModuloAuditoria) => {
    return await get<any>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/${modulo}/stats`)
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🚚 MIGRACIÓN
  // ============================================

  const migrarPDFs = async () => {
    return await post<any>(`${baseUrl}/auditoria/migrar-pdfs`, {})
  }

  return {
    // Público
    getDocumentosPublicos,
    // Admin - Documentos
    getDocumentosAdmin,
    getDocumentoById,
    createDocumento,
    updateDocumento,
    deleteDocumento,
    // Admin - Categorías
    getCategorias,
    createCategoria,
    updateCategoria,
    deleteCategoria,
    // Admin - POA-UAI
    getPOAs,
    getPOAById,
    createPOA,
    updatePOA,
    deletePOA,
    togglePOAActivo,
    // Uploads
    uploadPDF,
    // Stats
    getStats,
    // Migración
    migrarPDFs,
  }
}

// composables/useCatalogos.ts
import { useApi } from './useApi'

export interface SenadorCatalogo {
  id: number
  name: string
  tipo: 'titular' | 'suplente'
  party: string
  partyShort: string
  department: string
  foto: string
  cargo: string
  value: string   // = name (lo que se guarda en peticionario/comisionI/comisionII)
  label: string
}

export interface ComisionCatalogo {
  value: string
  label: string
}

export const useCatalogos = () => {
  const { get } = useApi()
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const baseUrl = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

  /**
   * Lista de senadores (titulares + suplentes).
   * @param q término de búsqueda opcional (nombre, partido, departamento)
   */
  const getSenadores = async (q?: string): Promise<SenadorCatalogo[]> => {
    const qs = q && q.trim() ? `?q=${encodeURIComponent(q.trim())}` : ''
    const result = await get<{ data: SenadorCatalogo[]; total: number }>(
      `${baseUrl}/catalogos/senadores${qs}`
    )
    return result.data || []
  }

  /**
   * Lista de comisiones camarales.
   */
  const getComisiones = async (): Promise<ComisionCatalogo[]> => {
    const result = await get<{ data: ComisionCatalogo[]; total: number }>(
      `${baseUrl}/catalogos/comisiones`
    )
    return result.data || []
  }

  return {
    getSenadores,
    getComisiones
  }
}

// composables/useComunicados.ts
import { useApi } from './useApi'

export interface Comunicado {
  _id: string
  titulo: string
  contenido: string
  imagen: {
    url: string
    alt: string
    name: string
  }
  pdf: {
    url: string | null
    name: string
    size: number
  }
  estado: 'programado' | 'activo' | 'inactivo'
  fechaActivacion: string | null
  fechaDesactivacion: string | null
  activadoEn: string | null
  desactivadoEn: string | null
  fechaLanzamiento: string
  prioridad: number
  creadoPor?: any
  actualizadoPor?: any
  createdAt: string
  updatedAt: string
}

export interface ComunicadoStats {
  total: number
  activos: number
  inactivos: number
  programados: number
  expirados: number
}

export const useComunicados = () => {
  const { get, post, put, del } = useApi()
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const baseUrl = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

  // ============================================
  // 📡 PÚBLICO
  // ============================================

  const getComunicadoActivo = async (): Promise<Comunicado | null> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/activo`)
      const result = await response.json()
      return result.data
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error obteniendo comunicado activo:', error)
      return null
    }
  }

  const getComunicadosExpirados = async (): Promise<Comunicado[]> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/expirados`)
      const result = await response.json()
      return result.data || []
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error obteniendo comunicados expirados:', error)
      return []
    }
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🔐 ADMIN
  // ============================================

  const getComunicados = async (params?: {
    page?: number
    limit?: number
    estado?: string
    search?: string
  }) => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams()
    if (params?.page) query.append('page', params.page.toString())
    if (params?.limit) query.append('limit', params.limit.toString())
    if (params?.estado && params.estado !== 'todos') query.append('estado', params.estado)
    if (params?.search) query.append('search', params.search)

    const url = `${baseUrl}/comunicados?${query.toString()}`
    return await get<any>(url)
  }

  const getComunicadoById = async (id: string): Promise<Comunicado> => {
    return await get<Comunicado>(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/${id}`)
  }

  const createComunicado = async (data: Partial<Comunicado>) => {
    return await post<Comunicado>(`${baseUrl}/comunicados`, data)
  }

  const updateComunicado = async (id: string, data: Partial<Comunicado>) => {
    return await put<Comunicado>(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/${id}`, data)
  }

  /**
   * 🔥 Cambiar estado con manejo de errores mejorado
   * El backend valida según las reglas (ej: no puedes activar un programado sin fechas)
   */
  const changeEstado = async (id: string, estado: string) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/${id}/estado`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ estado })
    })

    const result = await response.json()

    if (!response.ok || !result.success) {
      // Lanzar el mensaje real del backend
      throw new Error(result.message || 'Error al cambiar estado')
    }

    return result.data
  }

  const deleteComunicado = async (id: string) => {
    return await del(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/${id}`)
  }

  const getStats = async (): Promise<ComunicadoStats> => {
    return await get<ComunicadoStats>(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/stats`)
  }

  const forceUpdate = async () => {
    return await post(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/force-update`, {})
  }

  const uploadImage = async (file: File, name?: string): Promise<any> => {
    const formData = new FormData()
    formData.append('imagen', file)
    if (name) formData.append('name', name)

    const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/upload/image`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
      body: formData
    })
    const result = await response.json()
    if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.message)
    return result.data
  }

  const uploadPDF = async (file: File): Promise<any> => {
    const formData = new FormData()
    formData.append('pdf', file)

    const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/comunicados/upload/pdf`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
      body: formData
    })
    const result = await response.json()
    if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.message)
    return result.data
  }

  return {
    // Público
    getComunicadoActivo,
    getComunicadosExpirados,
    // Admin
    getComunicados,
    getComunicadoById,
    createComunicado,
    updateComunicado,
    changeEstado,
    deleteComunicado,
    getStats,
    forceUpdate,
    uploadImage,
    uploadPDF
  }
}

composables\useDocumentosLegislativos.ts

// composables/useDocumentosLegislativos.ts
import { useApi } from './useApi'
import type { TipoDocumento } from '~/utils/documentoTypes'

export interface ArchivoAnexo {
  url: string
  filename: string
  originalName: string
  size: number
}

export interface DocumentoLegislativo {
  _id: string
  tipo: TipoDocumento
  numero: number
  codigo: string
  fechaIngreso: string

  // 🔥 AHORA SON ARRAYS
  peticionario?: string[]
  comisionI?: string[]
  comisionII?: string[]

  // Compat: por si viene el nombre viejo
  proyectista?: string | string[]

  asunto: string
  destinatario?: string
  tipoDestinatario?: string
  fechaRemisionDestinatario?: string | null
  fechaRespuesta?: string | null
  fechaRemisionPeticionario?: string | null
  conminatoria?: string
  fechaRealizacion?: string | null
  fechaReprogramacion?: string | null
  fechaRealizacionFinal?: string | null
  fechaAprobacion?: string | null
  sesion?: string | null
  observacion?: string
  archivo: {
    url: string | null
    filename: string
    originalName: string
    size: number
  }

  archivoAnexo?: ArchivoAnexo[]
  numeroLey?: number | null
  fechaRechazo?: string | null
  esNuevoOReReposicion?: string
  plAnterior?: string
  fechaRemisionComisionI?: string | null
  fechaRemisionComisionII?: string | null
  estadoLey?: string
  fechaRemisionDiputados?: string | null
  fechaSancion?: string | null
  fechaRemisionEjecutivo?: string | null

  activo: boolean
  eliminado: boolean
  eliminadoPor?: any
  fechaEliminacion?: string | null
  motivoEliminacion?: string
  creadoPor?: any
  actualizadoPor?: any
  createdAt: string
  updatedAt: string
}

export interface PaginatedResult {
  documentos: DocumentoLegislativo[]
  total: number
  page: number
  limit: number
  pages: number
}

export interface StatsResult {
  total: number
  activos: number
  eliminados: number
}

export interface ConfigTipo {
  tipo: TipoDocumento
  label: string
  bloque: string
  prefijoCodigo: string
  campos: string[]
  camposObligatorios: string[]
  camposBloqueadosEnEdicion: string[]
  tiposDestinatario: string[]
}

export interface UploadResult {
  url: string
  filename: string
  originalName: string
  size: number
  sizeMB: string
}

/**
 * Normaliza un campo que debe ser array de strings.
 * Acepta string, array, null, undefined.
 */
function toArray(value: unknown): string[] {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value.map((v) => String(v).trim()).filter(Boolean)
  }
  if (typeof value === 'string') {
    const v = value.trim()
    return v ? [v] : []
  }
  return []
}

/**
 * Normaliza un documento que viene del backend para asegurar
 * que peticionario / comisionI / comisionII sean arrays de strings.
 */
function normalizarDocumento(doc: any): DocumentoLegislativo {
  if (!doc) return doc

  // peticionario puede venir como peticionario o proyectista
  const peticionario = toArray(doc.peticionario ?? doc.proyectista)

  return {
    ...doc,
    peticionario,
    comisionI: toArray(doc.comisionI),
    comisionII: toArray(doc.comisionII),
    archivoAnexo: Array.isArray(doc.archivoAnexo) ? doc.archivoAnexo : []
  }
}

export const useDocumentosLegislativos = () => {
  const { get, post, put, patch } = useApi()
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const baseUrl = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

  // ============================================
  // 📡 PÚBLICO
  // ============================================
  const getDocumentosPublicos = async (
    tipo: TipoDocumento,
    params?: { page?: number; limit?: number; search?: string; gestion?: number }
  ): Promise<PaginatedResult> => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams()
    if (params?.page) query.append('page', String(params.page))
    if (params?.limit) query.append('limit', String(params.limit))
    if (params?.search) query.append('search', params.search)
    if (params?.gestion) query.append('gestion', String(params.gestion))

    const qs = query.toString()
    const url = `${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`
    const result = await get<PaginatedResult>(url)

    // Normalizar arrays
    return {
      ...result,
      documentos: (result.documentos || []).map(normalizarDocumento)
    }
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🔐 ADMIN
  // ============================================
  const getDocumentosAdmin = async (
    tipo: TipoDocumento,
    params?: {
      page?: number
      limit?: number
      search?: string
      gestion?: number
      incluirInactivos?: boolean
      incluirEliminados?: boolean
    }
  ): Promise<PaginatedResult> => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams()
    if (params?.page) query.append('page', String(params.page))
    if (params?.limit) query.append('limit', String(params.limit))
    if (params?.search) query.append('search', params.search)
    if (params?.gestion) query.append('gestion', String(params.gestion))
    if (params?.incluirInactivos) query.append('incluirInactivos', 'true')
    if (params?.incluirEliminados) query.append('incluirEliminados', 'true')

    const qs = query.toString()
    const url = `${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}/admin${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`
    const result = await get<PaginatedResult>(url)

    return {
      ...result,
      documentos: (result.documentos || []).map(normalizarDocumento)
    }
  }

  const getDocumentoById = async (
    tipo: TipoDocumento,
    id: string
  ): Promise<DocumentoLegislativo> => {
    const result = await get<DocumentoLegislativo>(
      `${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}/${id}`
    )
    return normalizarDocumento(result)
  }

  const createDocumento = async (
    tipo: TipoDocumento,
    data: Partial<DocumentoLegislativo>
  ): Promise<DocumentoLegislativo> => {
    const result = await post<DocumentoLegislativo>(
      `${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}`,
      data
    )
    return normalizarDocumento(result)
  }

  const updateDocumento = async (
    tipo: TipoDocumento,
    id: string,
    data: Partial<DocumentoLegislativo>
  ): Promise<DocumentoLegislativo> => {
    const result = await put<DocumentoLegislativo>(
      `${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}/${id}`,
      data
    )
    return normalizarDocumento(result)
  }

  const deleteDocumento = async (
    tipo: TipoDocumento,
    id: string,
    motivo: string
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}/${id}`, {
      method: 'DELETE',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ motivo })
    })
    const result = await response.json()
    if (!response.ok || !result.success) {
      throw new Error(result.message || 'Error al eliminar el documento')
    }
    return result
  }

  const restoreDocumento = async (
    tipo: TipoDocumento,
    id: string
  ): Promise<DocumentoLegislativo> => {
    const result = await patch<DocumentoLegislativo>(
      `${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}/${id}/restore`,
      {}
    )
    return normalizarDocumento(result)
  }

  // ============================================
  // 📤 UPLOAD PDF
  // ============================================
  const uploadPDF = async (
    tipo: TipoDocumento,
    file: File
  ): Promise<UploadResult> => {
    const formData = new FormData()
    formData.append('pdf', file)

    const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}/upload-pdf`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      body: formData
    })
    const result = await response.json()
    if (!response.ok || !result.success) {
      throw new Error(result.message || 'Error al subir el PDF')
    }
    return result.data
  }

  // ============================================
  // 📊 STATS / CONFIG
  // ============================================
  const getStats = async (tipo: TipoDocumento): Promise<StatsResult> => {
    return await get<StatsResult>(`${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}/stats`)
  }

  const getConfigDelTipo = async (tipo: TipoDocumento): Promise<ConfigTipo> => {
    return await get<ConfigTipo>(`${baseUrl}/legislativos/${tipo}/config`)
  }

  return {
    getDocumentosPublicos,
    getDocumentosAdmin,
    getDocumentoById,
    createDocumento,
    updateDocumento,
    deleteDocumento,
    restoreDocumento,
    uploadPDF,
    getStats,
    getConfigDelTipo
  }
}

composables\useLegislativosCatalogos.ts

// composables/useLegislativosCatalogos.ts
import { useApi } from './useApi'

export interface CatalogoItem {
  _id: string
  id: string
  nombre: string
  name: string
  value: string
  label: string
  activo: boolean
  orden: number
}

export interface CreateCatalogoPayload {
  nombre: string
  activo?: boolean
  orden?: number
}

export interface UpdateCatalogoPayload {
  nombre?: string
  activo?: boolean
  orden?: number
}

export const useLegislativosCatalogos = () => {
  const { get, post, put, del } = useApi()
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const baseUrl = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

  // ============================================
  // 📡 LECTURA
  // ============================================
  const getSenadores = async (params?: {
    q?: string
    incluirInactivos?: boolean
  }): Promise<CatalogoItem[]> => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams()
    if (params?.q) query.append('q', params.q)
    if (params?.incluirInactivos) query.append('incluirInactivos', 'true')

    const qs = query.toString()
    const url = `${baseUrl}/legislativos/catalogos/senadores${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`
    
    // 🔥 CORRECCIÓN: useApi ya devuelve data.data, así que result ES el array
    const result = await get<any>(url)
    
    // Si el backend devuelve el objeto completo por alguna razón, lo atajamos:
    if (result && Array.isArray(result.data)) return result.data
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result
    
    return []
  }

  const getComisiones = async (params?: {
    q?: string
    incluirInactivos?: boolean
  }): Promise<CatalogoItem[]> => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams()
    if (params?.q) query.append('q', params.q)
    if (params?.incluirInactivos) query.append('incluirInactivos', 'true')

    const qs = query.toString()
    const url = `${baseUrl}/legislativos/catalogos/comisiones${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`
    
    const result = await get<any>(url)
    if (result && Array.isArray(result.data)) return result.data
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result
    
    return []
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🔐 CRUD - SENADORES
  // ============================================
  const createSenador = async (payload: CreateCatalogoPayload): Promise<CatalogoItem> => {
    return await post<CatalogoItem>(
      `${baseUrl}/legislativos/catalogos/senadores`,
      payload
    )
  }

  const updateSenador = async (
    id: string,
    payload: UpdateCatalogoPayload
  ): Promise<CatalogoItem> => {
    return await put<CatalogoItem>(
      `${baseUrl}/legislativos/catalogos/senadores/${id}`,
      payload
    )
  }

  const deleteSenador = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
    await del(`${baseUrl}/legislativos/catalogos/senadores/${id}`)
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🔐 CRUD - COMISIONES
  // ============================================
  const createComision = async (payload: CreateCatalogoPayload): Promise<CatalogoItem> => {
    return await post<CatalogoItem>(
      `${baseUrl}/legislativos/catalogos/comisiones`,
      payload
    )
  }

  const updateComision = async (
    id: string,
    payload: UpdateCatalogoPayload
  ): Promise<CatalogoItem> => {
    return await put<CatalogoItem>(
      `${baseUrl}/legislativos/catalogos/comisiones/${id}`,
      payload
    )
  }

  const deleteComision = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
    await del(`${baseUrl}/legislativos/catalogos/comisiones/${id}`)
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🌱 SEED
  // ============================================
  const seedCatalogos = async (sobrescribir = false): Promise<{
    senadores: number
    comisiones: number
  }> => {
    return await post<{ senadores: number; comisiones: number }>(
      `${baseUrl}/legislativos/catalogos/seed`,
      { sobrescribir }
    )
  }

  return {
    getSenadores,
    getComisiones,
    createSenador,
    updateSenador,
    deleteSenador,
    createComision,
    updateComision,
    deleteComision,
    seedCatalogos
  }
} 
================================= 
composables\useNews.ts 
================================= 
 
// composables/useNews.ts
import { useApi } from './useApi'

export interface NewsItem {
  _id: string
  title: string
  slug: string
  content: string
  blocks: any[]
  excerpt: string
  type: 'news' | 'article' | 'announcement' | 'page'
  category: 'noticia' | 'importante'
  tags: string[]
  status: 'draft' | 'published' | 'archived' | 'scheduled'
  featuredImage?: {
    url: string
    alt: string
    name?: string
    caption?: string
  }
  gallery?: {
    url: string
    alt: string
    name: string
    caption: string
    order: number
  }[]
  participantes?: number[] // 🔥 NUEVO: IDs de los senadores participantes
  publishedAt?: string
  scheduledFor?: string
  views: number
  author: {
    _id: string
    email: string
    profile?: {
      firstName?: string
      lastName?: string
    }
  }
  createdAt: string
  updatedAt: string
}

export const useNews = () => {
  const { get, post, put, del } = useApi()
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const baseUrl = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

  const getNews = async (params?: {
    page?: number
    limit?: number
    status?: 'all' | 'published' | 'draft' | 'archived' | 'scheduled'
    type?: string
    category?: string
    search?: string
  }) => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams()
    if (params?.page) query.append('page', params.page.toString())
    if (params?.limit) query.append('limit', params.limit.toString())
    if (params?.status && params.status !== 'all') query.append('status', params.status)
    if (params?.type && params.type !== 'all') query.append('type', params.type)
    if (params?.category && params.category !== 'all') query.append('category', params.category)
    if (params?.search) query.append('search', params.search)
    
    const url = `${baseUrl}/content/admin?${query.toString()}`
    const result = await get<any>(url)
    return result
  }

  const getNewsById = async (id: string) => {
    const result = await get<NewsItem>(`${baseUrl}/content/${id}`)
    return result
  }

  const createNews = async (data: Partial<NewsItem>) => {
    const result = await post<NewsItem>(`${baseUrl}/content`, data)
    return result
  }

  const updateNews = async (id: string, data: Partial<NewsItem>) => {
    const result = await put<NewsItem>(`${baseUrl}/content/${id}`, data)
    return result
  }

  const deleteNews = async (id: string) => {
    const result = await del(`${baseUrl}/content/${id}`)
    return result
  }

  const changeStatus = async (id: string, status: string) => {
    const result = await put(`${baseUrl}/content/${id}/status`, { status })
    return result
  }

  const uploadImage = async (file: File, name?: string, alt?: string) => {
    const formData = new FormData()
    formData.append('image', file)
    if (name) formData.append('name', name)
    if (alt) formData.append('alt', alt)

    const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/content/upload/image`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
      body: formData
    })

    const result = await response.json()
    if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.message)
    return result.data
  }

  const getStats = async () => {
    const result = await get<any>(`${baseUrl}/content/stats`)
    return result
  }

  const getContentTypes = async () => {
    const result = await get<any[]>(`${baseUrl}/content/types`)
    return result
  }

  const getCategories = async () => {
    const result = await get<any[]>(`${baseUrl}/content/categories`)
    return result
  }

  return {
    getNews,
    getNewsById,
    createNews,
    updateNews,
    deleteNews,
    changeStatus,
    uploadImage,
    getStats,
    getContentTypes,
    getCategories
  }
} 
================================= 
composables\usePermissions.ts 
================================= 
 
// composables/usePermissions.ts
// Azúcar para las páginas: expone la matriz de utils/roles.ts ya atada al
// usuario logueado.
import { computed } from 'vue'
import { useAuthStore } from '~/stores/auth'
import {
  assignableRoles as assignableRolesFor,
  can as canPerform,
  canView as canViewSection,
  type ActionId,
  type SectionId,
} from '~/utils/roles'

export const usePermissions = () => {
  const auth = useAuthStore()

  const role = computed(() => auth.role)

  return {
    /** Rol efectivo del usuario logueado (null si no hay sesión válida) */
    role,
    roleLabel: computed(() => auth.roleLabel),
    isAdmin: computed(() => auth.isAdmin),
    isSuperAdmin: computed(() => auth.isSuperAdmin),
    hasPanelAccess: computed(() => auth.hasPanelAccess),
    /** Secciones visibles, en orden de menú */
    sections: computed(() => auth.sections),
    /** Roles que este usuario puede asignar a otros */
    assignableRoles: computed(() => assignableRolesFor(role.value)),

    canView: (section: SectionId) => canViewSection(role.value, section),
    can: (section: SectionId, action: ActionId = 'view') =>
      canPerform(role.value, section, action),
  }
}
 
================================= 
composables\useSenadores.ts 
================================= 
 
// composables/useSenadores.ts
import { senadores } from '~/data/senadores'

export interface Senador {
  id: number
  seatNumber: number
  name: string
  slug: string
  party: string
  partyShort: string
  partyColor: string
  department: string
  foto: string
}

export const useSenadores = () => {
  // Obtener todos los senadores para el select
  const getSenadoresList = (): Senador[] => {
    return senadores.map(s => ({
      id: s.id,
      seatNumber: s.seatNumber,
      name: s.name,
      slug: s.slug,
      party: s.party,
      partyShort: s.partyShort,
      partyColor: s.partyColor,
      department: s.department,
      foto: s.foto || '/images/default-avatar.png'
    }))
  }

  // Buscar senador por ID
  const getSenadorById = (id: number): Senador | null => {
    const senador = senadores.find(s => s.id === id)
    if (!senador) return null
    return {
      id: senador.id,
      seatNumber: senador.seatNumber,
      name: senador.name,
      slug: senador.slug,
      party: senador.party,
      partyShort: senador.partyShort,
      partyColor: senador.partyColor,
      department: senador.department,
      foto: senador.foto || '/images/default-avatar.png'
    }
  }

  // Buscar senadores por departamento
  const getSenadoresByDepartment = (department: string): Senador[] => {
    return senadores
      .filter(s => s.department === department)
      .map(s => ({
        id: s.id,
        seatNumber: s.seatNumber,
        name: s.name,
        slug: s.slug,
        party: s.party,
        partyShort: s.partyShort,
        partyColor: s.partyColor,
        department: s.department,
        foto: s.foto || '/images/default-avatar.png'
      }))
  }

  return {
    getSenadoresList,
    getSenadorById,
    getSenadoresByDepartment
  }
}

composables\useTiposDestinatario.ts

// composables/useTiposDestinatario.ts
import { useApi } from './useApi'

export interface TipoDestinatarioItem {
  _id: string
  id: string
  nombre: string
  name: string
  value: string
  label: string
  activo: boolean
  orden: number
}

export interface CreateTipoDestinatarioPayload {
  nombre: string
  activo?: boolean
  orden?: number
}

export interface UpdateTipoDestinatarioPayload {
  nombre?: string
  activo?: boolean
  orden?: number
}

export const useTiposDestinatario = () => {
  const { get, post, put, del } = useApi()
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const baseUrl = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

  // ============================================
  // 📡 LECTURA (pública)
  // ============================================
  const getTiposDestinatario = async (params?: {
    q?: string
    incluirInactivos?: boolean
  }): Promise<TipoDestinatarioItem[]> => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams()
    if (params?.q) query.append('q', params.q)
    if (params?.incluirInactivos) query.append('incluirInactivos', 'true')

    const qs = query.toString()
    const url = `${baseUrl}/tipos-destinatario${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`

    const result = await get<any>(url)
    if (result && Array.isArray(result.data)) return result.data
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result
    return []
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🔐 CRUD (admin)
  // ============================================
  const createTipoDestinatario = async (
    payload: CreateTipoDestinatarioPayload
  ): Promise<TipoDestinatarioItem> => {
    return await post<TipoDestinatarioItem>(
      `${baseUrl}/tipos-destinatario`,
      payload
    )
  }

  const updateTipoDestinatario = async (
    id: string,
    payload: UpdateTipoDestinatarioPayload
  ): Promise<TipoDestinatarioItem> => {
    return await put<TipoDestinatarioItem>(
      `${baseUrl}/tipos-destinatario/${id}`,
      payload
    )
  }

  const deleteTipoDestinatario = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
    await del(`${baseUrl}/tipos-destinatario/${id}`)
  }

  // ============================================
  // 🌱 SEED
  // ============================================
  const seedTiposDestinatario = async (sobrescribir = false) => {
    return await post<{ tiposDestinatario: number }>(
      `${baseUrl}/tipos-destinatario/seed`,
      { sobrescribir }
    )
  }

  return {
    getTiposDestinatario,
    createTipoDestinatario,
    updateTipoDestinatario,
    deleteTipoDestinatario,
    seedTiposDestinatario,
  }
} 
================================= 
composables\useUsers.ts 
================================= 
 
// composables/useUsers.ts
// Cliente del endpoint /api/users del backend.
// El backend ya valida permisos: crear/listar/editar → SUPER_ADMIN y ADMIN
// (listar también EDITOR), eliminar → solo SUPER_ADMIN.
import { useApi } from './useApi'
import type { Role, User, UserProfile, UserStatus } from '~/types/auth'

export interface UserListResult {
  users: User[]
  total: number
  pages: number
  page: number
  limit: number
}

export interface UserListParams {
  page?: number
  limit?: number
  role?: Role | 'todos'
  status?: UserStatus | 'todos'
  search?: string
}

export interface CreateUserPayload {
  email: string
  password: string
  role: Role
  status: UserStatus
  profile?: UserProfile
}

export interface UpdateUserPayload {
  role?: Role
  status?: UserStatus
  profile?: UserProfile
}

/** El login devuelve `id` y Mongo devuelve `_id`: unificamos. */
export function userIdOf(user: User | null | undefined): string {
  if (!user) return ''
  return String(user._id || user.id || '')
}

export const useUsers = () => {
  const { get, post, put, del } = useApi()
  const config = useRuntimeConfig()
  const baseUrl = config.public.apiUrl || 'https://demoback.senado.gob.bo/api'

  const getUsers = async (params?: UserListParams): Promise<UserListResult> => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams()
    if (params?.page) query.append('page', String(params.page))
    if (params?.limit) query.append('limit', String(params.limit))
    if (params?.role && params.role !== 'todos') query.append('role', params.role)
    if (params?.status && params.status !== 'todos') query.append('status', params.status)
    if (params?.search) query.append('search', params.search)

    const url = `${baseUrl}/users?${query.toString()}`
    return await get<UserListResult>(url)
  }

  const getUserById = async (id: string): Promise<User> => {
    return await get<User>(`${baseUrl}/users/${id}`)
  }

  const createUser = async (payload: CreateUserPayload): Promise<User> => {
    return await post<User>(`${baseUrl}/users`, payload)
  }

  const updateUser = async (id: string, payload: UpdateUserPayload): Promise<User> => {
    return await put<User>(`${baseUrl}/users/${id}`, payload)
  }

  const deleteUser = async (id: string) => {
    return await del<{ message?: string }>(`${baseUrl}/users/${id}`)
  }

  return {
    getUsers,
    getUserById,
    createUser,
    updateUser,
    deleteUser,
  }
}

// data/senadores.js - TODOS los senadores (Titulares + Suplentes)

const generarSlug = (nombre) => {
  if (!nombre) return ''
  return nombre
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/ñ/g, 'n')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s-]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/\s+/g, '-')
}

                >
                  Cerrar sesión
                </button>

            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

    <!-- Contenido -->
    <div class="flex">
      <main class="flex-1">
        <div class="py-6">
          <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <nav class="flex mb-6" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
              <ol class="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <li>
                  <div class="flex items-center">
                    <NuxtLink :to="authStore.homeRoute" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-500">
                      Inicio
                    </NuxtLink>
                  </div>
                </li>
                <template v-if="currentRoute !== authStore.homeRoute">
                  <li>
                    <div class="flex items-center">
                      <svg class="h-5 w-5 text-gray-400" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
                        <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M7.293 14.707a1 1 0 010-1.414L10.586 10 7.293 6.707a1 1 0 011.414-1.414l4 4a1 1 0 010 1.414l-4 4a1 1 0 01-1.414 0z" clip-rule="evenodd" />
                      </svg>
                      <span class="ml-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
                        {{ currentSection?.label || 'Panel' }}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </li>
                </template>
              </ol>
            </nav>

            <h1 class="text-2xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-6">
              {{ pageTitle }}
            </h1>

            <div class="bg-white shadow rounded-lg">
              <div class="px-4 py-5 sm:p-6">
                <slot />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>

    <footer class="bg-white border-t border-gray-200">
      <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto py-4 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
          <p class="text-sm text-gray-500">
            © {{ new Date().getFullYear() }} Senado de Bolivia. Todos los derechos reservados.
          </p>
          <p class="text-sm text-gray-500">
            v1.0.0
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onUnmounted, watch } from 'vue'
import { useAuthStore } from '~/stores/auth'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import { roleInfo, sectionForPath } from '~/utils/roles'

const authStore = useAuthStore()
const userMenuOpen = ref(false)

const route = useRoute()
const currentRoute = computed(() => route.path)

const currentSection = computed(() => sectionForPath(currentRoute.value))
const pageTitle = computed(() => currentSection.value?.title || 'Panel de Administración')
const roleBadgeClass = computed(
  () => roleInfo(authStore.role)?.badgeClass || 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700'
)

const isActiveRoute = (path: string) => {
  return currentRoute.value.startsWith(path)
}

const toggleUserMenu = () => {
  userMenuOpen.value = !userMenuOpen.value
}

const logout = () => {
  authStore.logout()
}

const showComingSoon = (feature: string) => {
  alert(`🚧 ${feature} - Próximamente\n\nEsta funcionalidad estará disponible en la siguiente versión.`)
  userMenuOpen.value = false
}

const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
  const target = event.target as HTMLElement
  if (!target.closest('.relative')) {
    userMenuOpen.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  authStore.initialize()
  document.addEventListener('click', handleClickOutside)
})

onUnmounted(() => {
  document.removeEventListener('click', handleClickOutside)
})

watch(currentRoute, (newPath) => {
  console.log('Ruta actual:', newPath, '| Sección:', currentSection.value?.id || 'ninguna')
})
</script>

middleware\auth.ts 
================================= 
 
// middleware/auth.ts
// Control de acceso del panel:
//   1. sin sesión            → /auth/login
//   2. con sesión pero sin rol válido  → /acceso-denegado (hay que volver a entrar)
//   3. rol sin acceso al panel         → /acceso-denegado
//   4. rol sin acceso a esa sección    → /acceso-denegado?seccion=...
import {
  canView,
  hasPanelAccess,
  homeRouteFor,
  normalizeRole,
  sectionForPath,
} from '~/utils/roles'
import type { Role } from '~/types/auth'

export default defineNuxtRouteMiddleware((to) => {
  // Solo en cliente
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return

  const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
  const rawUser = localStorage.getItem('user')

  let role: Role | null = null
  if (rawUser) {
    try {
      role = normalizeRole(JSON.parse(rawUser)?.role)
    } catch {
      role = null
    }
  }

  const isAuthRoute = to.path.startsWith('/auth')
  const isAdminRoute = to.path.startsWith('/admin')

  console.log('🔐 [auth] Ruta:', to.path, '| Token:', !!token, '| Rol:', role || 'desconocido')

  // Ya autenticado y entra a /auth → a su inicio según rol
  if (token && rawUser && isAuthRoute) {
    console.log('✅ [auth] Ya autenticado, redirigiendo a', homeRouteFor(role))
    return navigateTo(homeRouteFor(role))
  }

  if (!isAdminRoute) return

  // Rutas del panel sin sesión → login
  if (!token || !rawUser) {
    console.log('❌ [auth] Ruta protegida sin autenticación, redirigiendo a login')
    return navigateTo('/auth/login')
  }

  // Sesión sin rol reconocido (sesión vieja) → explicar y volver a entrar
  if (!role) {
    console.log('❌ [auth] Sesión sin rol válido')
    return navigateTo('/acceso-denegado')
  }

  // Rol sin ninguna sección habilitada (MODERATOR, VIEWER, CITIZEN)
  if (!hasPanelAccess(role)) {
    console.log('❌ [auth] El rol', role, 'no tiene acceso al panel')
    return navigateTo('/acceso-denegado')
  }

  // Sección puntual no permitida para su rol
  const section = sectionForPath(to.path)
  if (section && !canView(role, section.id)) {
    console.log('❌ [auth] El rol', role, 'no puede entrar a', section.id)
    return navigateTo(`/acceso-denegado?seccion=${section.id}`)
  }
})
 
================================= 
pages\acceso-denegado.vue 
================================= 
 
<!-- pages/acceso-denegado.vue -->
<template>
  <div>
    <div class="text-center">
      <div class="text-5xl mb-3">🚫</div>
      <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Acceso denegado</h3>
      <p class="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">
        {{ mensaje }}
      </p>
    </div>

    <div v-if="role" class="mt-6 rounded-md bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 p-4 text-left">
      <p class="text-sm text-gray-700">
        Tu rol es
        <span class="ml-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium" :class="roleBadge">
          {{ roleName }}
        </span>
      </p>
      <p class="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">{{ roleDescription }}</p>
    </div>

    <div v-if="seccionesPermitidas.length > 0" class="mt-6 text-left">
      <p class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Secciones que sí podés usar:</p>
      <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <NuxtLink
          v-for="section in seccionesPermitidas"
          :key="section.id"
          :to="section.path"
          class="inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1.5 text-sm bg-primary-50 text-primary-700 rounded-md hover:bg-primary-100"
        >
          <span class="mr-1">{{ section.icon }}</span>{{ section.label }}
        </NuxtLink>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-3">
      <button
        v-if="seccionesPermitidas.length > 0"
        @click="irAInicio"
        class="w-full py-2 px-4 rounded-md text-sm font-medium text-white bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700"
      >
        Ir a mi sección de inicio
      </button>
      <button
        @click="cerrarSesion"
        class="w-full py-2 px-4 rounded-md text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200"
      >
        Cerrar sesión
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRoute } from 'vue-router'
import { useAuthStore } from '~/stores/auth'
import { roleInfo, sectionById, type SectionId } from '~/utils/roles'

definePageMeta({
  layout: 'auth',
})

const route = useRoute()
const authStore = useAuthStore()

const seccionBloqueada = computed(() => {
  const raw = route.query.seccion
  const id = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw
  return id ? sectionById(id as SectionId) : null
})

const role = computed(() => authStore.role)
const roleName = computed(() => authStore.roleLabel)
const roleBadge = computed(() => roleInfo(authStore.role)?.badgeClass || 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700')
const roleDescription = computed(() => roleInfo(authStore.role)?.description || '')
const seccionesPermitidas = computed(() => authStore.sections)

const mensaje = computed(() => {
  if (authStore.needsRelogin) {
    return 'Tu sesión no tiene un rol válido asignado. Cerrá sesión y volvé a entrar para que se actualice.'
  }
  if (seccionBloqueada.value) {
    return `Tu rol no tiene permiso para entrar a "${seccionBloqueada.value.label}".`
  }
  return 'Tu rol no tiene ninguna sección habilitada en el panel de administración.'
})

const irAInicio = () => {
  authStore.goHome()
}

const cerrarSesion = () => {
  authStore.logout()
}

onMounted(() => {
  authStore.initialize()

  // Si no hay sesión, no tiene sentido mostrar esta pantalla
  const hasToken = !!localStorage.getItem('auth_token')
  if (!hasToken) {
    window.location.href = '/auth/login'
  }
})
</script>

          <div class="flex-1">
            <p class="font-medium text-sm">PDF cargado</p>
            <a :href="form.url" target="_blank" class="text-xs text-primary-600 hover:underline break-all">
              {{ form.url }}
            </a>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            @click="form.url = null"
            class="text-red-500 hover:text-red-700 text-xl flex-shrink-0"
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="flex justify-end space-x-3 pt-4 border-t">
        <NuxtLink
          to="/admin/auditoria"
          class="px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50"
        >
          Cancelar
        </NuxtLink>
        <button
          type="submit"
          :disabled="saving || !categorias.length"
          class="px-4 py-2 bg-primary-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-primary-700 disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <span
            v-if="saving"
            class="inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"
          ></span>
          {{ saving ? 'Guardando...' : 'Crear Documento' }}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref, reactive, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import {
  useAuditoria,
  type AuditoriaCategoria,
  type EstadoAuditoria,
} from '~/composables/useAuditoria'

definePageMeta({
  layout: 'default',
  middleware: 'auth',
})

// 🔥 Módulo fijo
const MODULO = 'auditorias-ejecutadas' as const

const router = useRouter()
const { createDocumento, uploadPDF, getCategorias } = useAuditoria()

const categorias = ref<AuditoriaCategoria[]>([])
const saving = ref(false)
const uploadingPDF = ref(false)
const uploadProgress = ref('')
const pdfInput = ref<HTMLInputElement | null>(null)

const form = reactive({
  categoria: '',
  titulo: '',
  descripcion: '',
  gestion: new Date().getFullYear(),
  estado: 'Publicado' as EstadoAuditoria,
  activo: true,
  orden: 0,
  url: null as string | null,
})

const loadCategorias = async () => {
  try {
    categorias.value = await getCategorias(MODULO, false)
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(err)
  }
}

const handlePdfSelect = async (event: Event) => {
  const target = event.target as HTMLInputElement
  const file = target.files?.[0]
  if (!file) return

  if (file.type !== 'application/pdf') {
    alert('Solo se permiten archivos PDF')
    return
  }

  uploadingPDF.value = true
  uploadProgress.value = 'Subiendo PDF...'

  try {
    const result = await uploadPDF(file, MODULO)
    form.url = result.url
    uploadProgress.value = '¡PDF subido con éxito!'
    setTimeout(() => (uploadProgress.value = ''), 3000)
  } catch (err: any) {
    alert(err.message || 'Error al subir el PDF')
    uploadProgress.value = ''
  } finally {
    uploadingPDF.value = false
    if (pdfInput.value) pdfInput.value.value = ''
  }
}

const saveDocumento = async () => {
  if (!form.categoria) {
    alert('Selecciona una categoría')
    return
  }
  if (!form.titulo.trim()) {
    alert('El título es requerido')
    return
  }
  if (!form.gestion) {
    alert('La gestión es requerida')
    return
  }

  saving.value = true
  try {
    await createDocumento(MODULO, {
      modulo: MODULO,
      categoria: form.categoria,
      titulo: form.titulo,
      descripcion: form.descripcion,
      gestion: form.gestion,
      estado: form.estado,
      activo: form.activo,
      orden: form.orden,
      url: form.url,
    })
    router.push('/admin/auditoria')
  } catch (err: any) {
    alert(err.message || 'Error al crear el documento')
  } finally {
    saving.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(loadCategorias)
</script> 
================================= 
pages\admin\auditoria\index.vue
